  │ 321 + def get_trace(process: Process, max_length: int = 10) -> Tuple[… │
  │ 322 +     """Get a trace (sequence of actions) from a process.   │
  │ 323 +   │
  │ 324 +     A trace is one possible execution path.   │
  │ 325 +     """   │

  │ 342 +     )   │
  │ 343 +   │
  │ 344 +     return trace, proof   │

  │ 347 + def trace_equivalence(p: Process, q: Process, max_depth: int = … │
  │ 348 +     """Check if p and q produce the same set of traces up to ma… │
  │ 349 +   │
  │ 350 +     This is a bounded check for trace equivalence.   │
  │ 351 +     Full trace equivalence requires exploring all possible trac… │

  │ 373 +     )   │
  │ 374 +   │
  │ 375 +     return equivalent, proof   │

  │ 118 + def check_happens_before(op_a: MemoryOperation,              │

  │ 121 +     """Check if operation a happens-before operation b.      │
  │ 122 +                                                              │
  │ 123 +     Happens-before relations:                                │
  │ 124 +     1. Program order: a and b in same thread, a precedes b   │
  │ 125 +     2. Synchronization: a is release, b is acquire, same lo… │
  │ 126 +     3. Transitivity: a hb b and b hb c implies a hb c        │
  │ 127 +                                                              │

  │ 134 +         (happens_before, proof)                              │

  │ 136 +     # Check program order (same thread)                      │
  │ 137 +     if op_a.thread_id == op_b.thread_id:                     │
  │ 138 +         thread_ops = program_order.get(op_a.thread_id, [])   │
  │ 139 +         try:                                                 │
  │ 140 +             idx_a = thread_ops.index(op_a)                   │
  │ 141 +             idx_b = thread_ops.index(op_b)                   │
  │ 142 +             if idx_a < idx_b:                                │
  │ 143 +                 return True, ProofObject(                    │
  │ 144 +                     rule="HappensBefore",                    │

  │ 146 +                     conclusion="happens_before via program … │

  │ 149 +             pass                                             │

  │ 154 +         op_a.address == op_b.address):                       │
  │ 155 +         return True, ProofObject(                            │
  │ 156 +             rule="HappensBefore",                            │
  │ 157 +             premises=["release-acquire pair", f"address={op… │
  │ 158 +             conclusion="happens_before via synchronization"  │
  │ 159 +         )                                                    │
  │ 160 +                                                              │
  │ 161 +     # Check if op_a timestamp is before op_b                 │
  │ 162 +     if op_a.timestamp < op_b.timestamp:                      │
  │ 163 +         return True, ProofObject(                            │
  │ 164 +             rule="HappensBefore",                            │

  │ 166 +             conclusion="happens_before via timestamp"        │
  │ 167 +         )                                                    │

  │ 173 +     )                                                        │

  │ 214 +                     continue                                 │
  │ 215 +                                                              │
  │ 216 +                 # Skip if neither is a write                 │
  │ 217 +                 if op_a.op_type not in [OpType.WRITE, OpTyp… │

  │ 324 + ) -> Tuple[bool, ProofObject]:                               │
  │ 325 +     """Check if execution respects Total Store Order (TSO).  │
  │ 326 +                                                              │
  │ 327 +     TSO (x86 memory model):                                  │
  │ 328 +     - Reads can be reordered after writes                    │
  │ 329 +     - Writes are totally ordered (store buffer flushed in o… │
  │ 330 +     - Each thread sees its own writes immediately            │
  │ 331 +                                                              │
  │ 332 +     Args:                                                    │
  │ 333 +         order: The memory order                              │
  │ 334 +         thread_ids: List of thread IDs in the system         │
  │ 335 +                                                              │
  │ 336 +     Returns:                                                 │
  │ 337 +         (is_tso, proof)                                      │
  │ 338 +     """                                                      │
  │ 339 +     # Simplified TSO check: verify no write-write reordering │
  │ 340 +     # In TSO, writes from the same thread must appear in pr… │
  │ 341 +                                                              │
  │ 342 +     violations = []                                          │
  │ 343 +                                                              │
  │ 344 +     for tid in thread_ids:                                   │
  │ 345 +         thread_writes = [                                    │
  │ 346 +             op for op in order.operations                    │
  │ 347 +             if op.thread_id == tid and op.op_type == OpType… │
  │ 348 +         ]                                                    │
  │ 349 +                                                              │
  │ 350 +         # Check writes appear in timestamp order             │
  │ 351 +         sorted_writes = sorted(thread_writes, key=lambda x:… │
  │ 352 +                                                              │
  │ 353 +         # Verify program order is respected                  │
  │ 354 +         for i, w in enumerate(thread_writes):                │
  │ 355 +             if i > 0:                                        │
  │ 356 +                 prev_w = thread_writes[i-1]                  │
  │ 357 +                 if w.timestamp < prev_w.timestamp:           │
  │ 358 +                     violations.append(                       │
  │ 359 +                         f"Thread {tid}: write reordering de… │
  │ 360 +                     )                                        │
  │ 361 +                                                              │
  │ 362 +     is_tso = len(violations) == 0                            │
  │ 363 +                                                              │
  │ 364 +     proof = ProofObject(                                     │
  │ 365 +         rule="TotalStoreOrder",                              │
  │ 366 +         premises=[                                           │
  │ 367 +             f"threads={len(thread_ids)}",                    │
  │ 368 +             f"operations={len(order.operations)}",           │
  │ 369 +             f"violations={len(violations)}"                  │
  │ 370 +         ],                                                   │
  │ 371 +         conclusion=f"is_tso={is_tso}"                        │
  │ 372 +     )                                                        │
  │ 373 +                                                              │
  │ 374 +     return is_tso, proof                                     │

  │ 276 +         A valid chain:                                       │

  │   1 + """Capability-Based Memory Manager.                          │
  │   2 +                                                              │
  │   3 + Memory regions are capabilities. No process can access memo… │
  │   4 + without holding the appropriate capability. Uses axioms/mem… │
  │   5 + for consistency guarantees.                                  │
  │   6 + """                                                          │
  │   7 +                                                              │
  │   8 + from __future__ import annotations                           │
  │   9 + from dataclasses import dataclass, field                     │
  │  10 + from typing import Tuple, List, Optional, Dict               │
  │  11 + from fractions import Fraction                               │
  │  12 +                                                              │
  │  13 + from axioms.logic import ProofObject                         │
  │  14 + from axioms.capability_security import Capability, Permissi… │
  │  15 +                                                              │
  │  16 +                                                              │
  │  17 + @dataclass(frozen=True)                                      │
  │  18 + class MemoryRegion:                                          │
  │  19 +     """A region of memory.                                   │
  │  20 +                                                              │
  │  21 +     Each region has an owner and is accessed via capabiliti… │
  │  22 +     """                                                      │
  │  23 +     region_id: str                                           │
  │  24 +     base: Fraction  # Base address                           │
  │  25 +     size: Fraction  # Size in bytes                          │
  │  26 +     owner: str      # Process ID of owner                    │
  │  27 +     permissions: frozenset  # Set of Permission              │
  │  28 +                                                              │
  │  29 +     def end(self) -> Fraction:                               │
  │  30 +         """Calculate end address (exclusive)."""             │
  │  31 +         return self.base + self.size                         │
  │  32 +                                                              │
  │  33 +     def contains(self, addr: Fraction) -> bool:              │
  │  34 +         """Check if address is within this region."""        │
  │  35 +         return self.base <= addr < self.end()                │
  │  36 +                                                              │
  │  37 +     def overlaps(self, other: MemoryRegion) -> bool:         │
  │  38 +         """Check if this region overlaps with another."""    │
  │  39 +         return not (self.end() <= other.base or other.end()… │
  │  40 +                                                              │
  │  41 +                                                              │
  │  42 + @dataclass                                                   │
  │  43 + class MemoryManagerState:                                    │
  │  44 +     """Complete state of the memory manager."""              │
  │  45 +     regions: List[MemoryRegion] = field(default_factory=lis… │
  │  46 +     free_list: List[Tuple[Fraction, Fraction]] = field(defa… │
  │  47 +     total: Fraction = field(default_factory=lambda: Fractio… │
  │  48 +                                                              │
  │  49 +     def allocated(self) -> Fraction:                         │
  │  50 +         """Calculate total allocated memory."""              │
  │  51 +         return sum(r.size for r in self.regions)             │
  │  52 +                                                              │
  │  53 +     def available(self) -> Fraction:                         │
  │  54 +         """Calculate available memory."""                    │
  │  55 +         return self.total - self.allocated()                 │
  │  56 +                                                              │
  │  57 +                                                              │
  │  58 + def allocate(state: MemoryManagerState,                      │
  │  59 +             requester: str,                                  │
  │  60 +             size: Fraction,                                  │
  │  61 +             permissions: frozenset) -> Tuple[MemoryManagerS… │
  │  62 +     """Allocate a memory region.                             │
  │  63 +                                                              │
  │  64 +     Uses first-fit from free list. Returns capability for n… │
  │  65 +                                                              │
  │  66 +     Args:                                                    │
  │  67 +         state: Current memory manager state                  │
  │  68 +         requester: Process requesting allocation             │
  │  69 +         size: Size in bytes to allocate                      │
  │  70 +         permissions: Permissions for the new region          │
  │  71 +                                                              │
  │  72 +     Returns:                                                 │
  │  73 +         (new_state, capability, proof)                       │
  │  74 +         If allocation fails, capability is None              │
  │  75 +     """                                                      │
  │  76 +     if size <= Fraction(0):                                  │

  │   1 + """Network Bridge — Capability-gated network access.         │
  │   2 +                                                              │
  │   3 + The kernel does not implement TCP/IP. It mediates access to  │

  │ 149 +             premises=[],                                     │
  │ 150 +             conclusion="translated"                          │
  │ 151 +         )                                                    │

  │ 270 + def boot(total_memory: Fraction) -> Tuple[BootState, ProofO… │
  │ 271 +     """Execute full boot sequence.                           │
  │ 272 +                                                              │
  │ 273 +     Each phase checks preconditions before executing.        │
  │ 274 +     Each phase records postconditions after executing.       │
  │ 275 +     The final proof is a chain of step proofs.               │
  │ 276 +                                                              │
  │ 277 +     Args:                                                    │
  │ 278 +         total_memory: Total memory available to the system   │
  │ 279 +                                                              │
  │ 280 +     Returns:                                                 │
  │ 281 +         (final_state, proof)                                 │
  │ 282 +     """                                                      │
  │ 283 +     state = BootState()                                      │
  │ 284 +                                                              │
  │ 285 +     # Execute boot sequence                                  │
  │ 286 +     state, proof_hal = boot_step_hal(state)                  │
  │ 287 +     state, proof_memory = boot_step_memory(state, total_mem… │
  │ 288 +     state, proof_scheduler = boot_step_scheduler(state)      │
  │ 289 +     state, proof_ipc = boot_step_ipc(state)                  │
  │ 290 +     state, proof_bridges = boot_step_bridges(state)          │
  │ 291 +     state, proof_userland = boot_step_userland(state)        │
  │ 292 +                                                              │
  │ 293 +     # Final proof aggregates all step proofs                 │
  │ 294 +     final_proof = ProofObject(                               │
  │ 295 +         rule="BootSequence",                                 │
  │ 296 +         premises=[                                           │
  │ 297 +             f"steps={len(state.steps_completed)}",           │
  │ 298 +             f"hal={state.hal_initialized}",                  │
  │ 299 +             f"memory={state.memory_initialized}",            │
  │ 300 +             f"scheduler={state.scheduler_initialized}",      │
  │ 301 +             f"ipc={state.ipc_initialized}",                  │
  │ 302 +             f"bridges={state.bridges_initialized}",          │
  │ 303 +             f"userland={state.userland_reached}"             │
  │ 304 +         ],                                                   │
  │ 305 +         conclusion="boot sequence complete"                  │
  │ 306 +     )                                                        │
  │ 307 +                                                              │
  │ 308 +     return state, final_proof                                │
  │ 309 +                                                              │
  │ 310 +                                                              │
  │ 311 + def verify_boot_integrity(state: BootState) -> Tuple[bool, … │
  │ 312 +     """Verify the boot sequence is complete and all postcon… │
  │ 313 +                                                              │
  │ 314 +     Args:                                                    │
  │ 315 +         state: Boot state to verify                          │
  │ 316 +                                                              │
  │ 317 +     Returns:                                                 │
  │ 318 +         (valid, proof)                                       │
  │ 319 +     """                                                      │
  │ 320 +     all_initialized = (                                      │
  │ 321 +         state.hal_initialized and                            │
  │ 322 +         state.memory_initialized and                         │
  │ 323 +         state.scheduler_initialized and                      │
  │ 324 +         state.ipc_initialized and                            │
  │ 325 +         state.bridges_initialized and                        │
  │ 326 +         state.userland_reached                               │
  │ 327 +     )                                                        │
  │ 328 +                                                              │
  │ 329 +     # Check phase ordering                                   │
  │ 330 +     expected_phases = [                                      │
  │ 331 +         BootPhase.HAL_INIT,                                  │
  │ 332 +         BootPhase.MEMORY_INIT,                               │
  │ 333 +         BootPhase.SCHEDULER_INIT,                            │
  │ 334 +         BootPhase.IPC_INIT,                                  │
  │ 335 +         BootPhase.BRIDGE_INIT,                               │
  │ 336 +         BootPhase.USERLAND                                   │
  │ 337 +     ]                                                        │
  │ 338 +                                                              │
  │ 339 +     phase_order_ok = len(state.steps_completed) == len(expe… │
  │ 340 +     if phase_order_ok:                                       │
  │ 341 +         for i, step in enumerate(state.steps_completed):     │
  │ 342 +             if step.phase != expected_phases[i]:             │
  │ 343 +                 phase_order_ok = False                       │
  │ 344 +                 break                                        │
  │ 345 +                                                              │
  │ 346 +     valid = all_initialized and phase_order_ok               │
  │ 347 +                                                              │
  │ 348 +     proof = ProofObject(                                     │
  │ 349 +         rule="BootIntegrity",                                │
  │ 350 +         premises=[                                           │
  │ 351 +             f"all_initialized={all_initialized}",            │
  │ 352 +             f"phase_order_ok={phase_order_ok}",              │
  │ 353 +             f"steps={len(state.steps_completed)}"            │
  │ 354 +         ],                                                   │
  │ 355 +         conclusion=f"valid={valid}"                          │
  │ 356 +     )                                                        │
  │ 357 +                                                              │
  │ 358 +     return valid, proof                                      │

  Design Principles:
